np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=np.int64)\n   291→    env_trial = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=np.int64)\n   292→    lick_error = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=bool)\n   293→    rz_onset_pos = np.full(n_trials, np.nan)\n   294→    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(zip(starts, stops)):\n   295→        sl = slice(s - 1, e - 1)\n   296→        rzflag = S['rzone_flag'][sl] > 0\n   297→        got_reward = np.any((reward_frames >= s - 1) & (reward_frames < e - 1))\n   298→        # behavior.get_trial_types: reward delivered AND reward zone active\n   299→        isreward[i] = int(got_reward and np.any(rzflag))\n   300→        env_trial[i] = int(np.round(np.median(S['env'][sl])))\n   301→        L = S['lick'][sl]\n   302→        lick_error[i] = (np.sum(L > 2) / len(L)) > LICK_ERROR_THRESH\n   303→        if np.any(rzflag):\n   304→            rz_onset_pos[i] = S['pos'][sl][np.argmax(rzflag)]\n   305→\n   306→    # sanity check: scene-derived zone vs zone entry position measured in the data\n   307→    obs = rz_onset_pos[~np.isnan(rz_onset_pos)]\n   308→    exp = rz_coords[~np.isnan(rz_onset_pos), 0]\n   309→    zone_err = float(np.nanmedian(obs - exp)) if len(obs) else np.nan\n   310→    if len(obs) and not (-1.0 <= zone_err <= 15.0):\n   311→        print('  WARNING %s: reward-zone mismatch, median(onset-zone_start)=%.1f cm'\n   312→              % (os.path.basename(fn), zone_err))\n   313→\n   314→    # ---- build per-trial arrays ----\n   315→    neural, inp, out = [], [], []\n   316→    kept_trials = []\n   317→    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(zip(starts, stops)):\n   318→        if i == 0:\n   319→            continue                      # previous-trial outcome undefined\n   320→        if lick_error[i]:\n   321→            continue                      # invalid lick data (reference sets to NaN)\n   322→        sl = slice(s - 1, e - 1)\n   323→        T = (e - 1) - (s - 1)\n   324→        ev = events[np.ix_(keep_cells, np.arange(s - 1, e - 1))]\n   325→        if not np.all(np.isfinite(ev)):\n   326→            print('  WARNING %s trial %d: non-finite events, trial dropped'\n   327→                  % (os.path.basename(fn), i))\n   328→            continue\n   329→\n   330→        pos = S['pos'][sl]\n   331→        speed = S['speed'][sl]\n   332→        lick = S['lick'][sl]\n   333→        tt = S['t'][sl] - S['t'][s]       # time from trial start (s)\n   334→\n   335→        x = np.empty((4, T), dtype=np.float32)\n   336→        x[0] = tt\n   337→        x[1] = env_trial[i]\n   338→        x[2] = i                           # lap index within the session\n   339→        x[3] = isreward[i - 1]             # previous trial outcome\n   340→\n   341→        d_rz = reward_zone_distance(pos, rz_coords[i, 0], rz_coords[i, 1])\n   342→        y = np.empty((6, T), dtype=np.int64)\n   343→        y[0] = digitize_rz_distance(d_rz)\n   344→        y[1] = np.digitize(pos, POSITION_EDGES)\n   345→        y[2] = np.digitize(speed, SPEED_EDGES)\n   346→        y[3] = (lick >= 1).astype(np.int64)\n   347→        y[4] = RZ_LABELS.index(rz_labels[i])\n   348→        y[5] = isreward[i]\n   349→\n   350→        neural.append(ev.astype(np.float32))\n   351→        inp.append(x)\n   352→        out.append(y)\n   353→        kept_trials.append(i)\n   354→\n   355→    sess = dict(\n   356→        neural=neural, input=inp, output=out,\n   357→        subject=S['subject'], session_id=S['session_id'], scene=S['scene'],\n   358→        date=S['date'], location=S['location'],\n   359→        n_neurons=int(keep_cells.sum()), kept_trials=np.array(kept_trials),\n   360→        dt=dt,\n   361→    )\n   362→    stats = dict(\n   363→        file=os.path.basename(fn), subject=S['subject'], session=S['session_id'],\n   364→        scene=S['scene'], n_roi=S['n_roi_total'], n_iscell=S['n_iscell'],\n   365→        n_interneuron=int(is_int.sum()), n_bad_baseline=int(bad_baseline.sum()),\n   366→        n_neurons=int(keep_cells.sum()),\n   367→        n_trials_raw=n_trials, n_trials_kept=len(neural),\n   368→        n_lick_error=int(lick_error.sum()), n_rewarded=int(isreward.sum()),\n   369→        frac_rewarded=float(isreward.mean()), zone_err=zone_err,\n   370→        dff_min=float(np.nanmin(dff[keep_cells])), dff_max=float(np.nanmax(dff[keep_cells])),\n   371→        ev_max=float(np.nanmax(events[keep_cells])), dt=dt,\n   372→        t_load=t_load, t_dff=t_dff, t_int=t_int, t_total=time.time() - t0,\n   373→    )\n   374→\n   375→    if show:\n   376→        try:\n   377→            make_processing_plots(S, dff, events, keep_cells, rz_coords, rz_labels,\n   378→                                 isreward, sess, plotdir)\n   379→        except Exception:\n   380→            traceback.print_exc()\n   381→\n   382→    return sess, stats\n   383→\n   384→\n   385→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   386→# visualisation of every processing step (--show-processing)\n   387→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   388→def make_processing_plots(S, dff, events, keep_cells, rz_coords, rz_labels,\n   389→                          isreward, sess, plotdir):\n   390→    import matplotlib\n   391→    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n   392→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n   393→\n   394→    sid = '%s_ses-%s' % (S['subject'], S['session_id'])\n   395→    starts, stops = S['starts'], S['stops']\n   396→    # a window covering trials 1-4 (0-indexed), which are all kept trials\n   397→    i0, i1 = 1, min(5, len(starts) - 1)\n   398→    a, b = starts[i0] - 1, stops[i1 - 1] - 1\n   399→    tt = S['t'][a:b]\n   400→    cells = np.where(keep_cells)[0][:5]\n   401→\n   402→    fig, ax = plt.subplots(7, 1, figsize=(14, 16), sharex=True)\n   403→    for c in cells:\n   404→        ax[0].plot(tt, S['F'][c, a:b], lw=0.7)\n   405→    ax[0].set_ylabel('raw F')\n   406→    ax[0].set_title('%s (%s): raw fluorescence of 5 example curated cells' % (sid, S['scene']))\n   407→    for c in cells:\n   408→        ax[1].plot(tt, S['Fneu'][c, a:b], lw=0.7)\n   409→    ax[1].set_ylabel('neuropil F')\n   410→    for c in cells:\n   411→        ax[2].plot(tt, dff[c, a:b], lw=0.7)\n   412→    ax[2].set_ylabel('dF/F')\n   413→    for c in cells:\n   414→        ax[3].plot(tt, events[c, a:b], lw=0.7)\n   415→    ax[3].set_ylabel('events (OASIS)')\n   416→    ax[4].plot(tt, S['pos'][a:b], 'k', lw=1)\n   417→    for i in range(i0, i1):\n   418→        s, e = starts[i] - 1, stops[i] - 1\n   419→        ax[4].axhspan(rz_coords[i, 0], rz_coords[i, 1], xmin=(s - a) / (b - a),\n   420→                      xmax=(e - a) / (b - a), color='g', alpha=0.2)\n   421→    ax[4].set_ylabel('position (cm)')\n   422→    ax[5].plot(tt, S['speed'][a:b], 'b', lw=1)\n   423→    ax[5].axhline(2, color='gray', ls=':')\n   424→    ax[5].set_ylabel('speed (cm/s)')\n   425→    ax[6].plot(tt, S['lick'][a:b], 'm', lw=1, label='cumulative lick count')\n   426→    rf = np.searchsorted(S['t'], S['reward_t'])\n   427→    rf = rf[(rf >= a) & (rf < b)]\n   428→    ax[6].plot(S['t'][rf], np.ones(len(rf)) * 0.5, 'r*', ms=12, label='reward')\n   429→    ax[6].set_ylabel('licks / reward')\n   430→    ax[6].legend(loc='upper right', fontsize=7)\n   431→    ax[6].set_xlabel('session time (s)')\n   432→    for axi in ax:\n   433→        for i in range(i0, i1 + 1):\n   434→            axi.axvline(S['t'][starts[i] - 1], color='g', lw=0.8)\n   435→            axi.axvline(S['t'][stops[i] - 1], color='r', lw=0.8, ls='--')\n   436→    fig.tight_layout()\n   437→    fig.savefig(os.path.join(plotdir, 'processing_%s_timeseries.png' % sid), dpi=110)\n   438→    plt.close(fig)\n   439→\n   440→    # ---- discretisation checks + per-trial alignment of converted arrays ----\n   441→    k = 0  # first kept trial\n   442→    x, y = sess['input'][k], sess['output'][k]\n   443→    trial = sess['kept_trials'][k]\n   444→    s, e = starts[trial] - 1, stops[trial] - 1\n   445→    pos = S['pos'][s:e]\n   446→    d_rz = reward_zone_distance(pos, rz_coords[trial, 0], rz_coords[trial, 1])\n   447→\n   448→    fig, ax = plt.subplots(4, 2, figsize=(14, 12))\n   449→    ax[0, 0].plot(x[0], pos, 'k')\n   450→    ax2 = ax[0, 0].twinx()\n   451→    ax2.step(x[0], y[1], 'r', where='post')\n   452→    ax[0, 0].axhline(rz_coords[trial, 0], color='g', ls=':')\n   453→    ax[0, 0].axhline(rz_coords[trial, 1], color='g', ls=':')\n   454→    ax[0, 0].set_xlabel('time from trial start (s)')\n   455→    ax[0, 0].set_ylabel('position (cm)')\n   456→    ax2.set_ylabel('position bin (red)')\n   457→    ax[0, 0].set_title('%s trial %d: position and its discretisation' % (sid, trial))\n   458→\n   459→    ax[0, 1].plot(x[0], d_rz, 'k')\n   460→    ax3 = ax[0, 1].twinx()\n   461→    ax3.step(x[0], y[0], 'r', where='post')\n   462→    ax[0, 1].set_xlabel('time from trial start (s)')\n   463→    ax[0, 1].set_ylabel('distance to reward zone (cm)')\n   464→    ax3.set_ylabel('distance bin (red)')\n   465→    ax[0, 1].set_title('distance to reward zone (0 inside zone %s)' % rz_labels[trial])\n   466→\n   467→    ax[1, 0].plot(x[0], S['speed'][s:e], 'b')\n   468→    ax4 = ax[1, 0].twinx()\n   469→    ax4.step(x[0], y[2], 'r', where='post')\n   470→    ax[1, 0].set_ylabel('speed (cm/s)')\n   471→    ax4.set_ylabel('speed bin (red)')\n   472→    ax[1, 0].set_xlabel('time from trial start (s)')\n   473→\n   474→    ax[1, 1].plot(x[0], S['lick'][s:e], 'm', label='cumulative count')\n   475→    ax[1, 1].step(x[0], y[3], 'r', where='post', label='lick output')\n   476→    ax[1, 1].legend(fontsize=7)\n   477→    ax[1, 1].set_xlabel('time from trial start (s)')\n   478→\n   479→    # discretisation scatter over the whole session\n   480→    allpos = np.concatenate([S['pos'][starts[i] - 1:stops[i] - 1] for i in sess['kept_trials']])\n   481→    allposbin = np.concatenate([o[1] for o in sess['output']])\n   482→    ax[2, 0].plot(allpos, allposbin, '.', ms=1)\n   483→    ax[2, 0].set_xlabel('position (cm)'); ax[2, 0].set_ylabel('bin')\n   484→    allsp = np.concatenate([S['speed'][starts[i] - 1:stops[i] - 1] for i in sess['kept_trials']])\n   485→    allspbin = np.concatenate([o[2] for o in sess['output']])\n   486→    ax[2, 1].plot(allsp, allspbin, '.', ms=1)\n   487→    ax[2, 1].set_xlabel('speed (cm/s)'); ax[2, 1].set_ylabel('bin')\n   488→    alld = np.concatenate([reward_zone_distance(S['pos'][starts[i] - 1:stops[i] - 1],\n   489→                                                rz_coords[i, 0], rz_coords[i, 1])\n   490→                           for i in sess['kept_trials']])\n   491→    alldbin = np.concatenate([o[0] for o in sess['output']])\n   492→    ax[3, 0].plot(alld, alldbin, '.', ms=1)\n   493→    ax[3, 0].set_xlabel('distance to reward zone (cm)'); ax[3, 0].set_ylabel('bin')\n   494→\n   495→    # neural raster of the converted trial next to position, to check alignment\n   496→    ev = sess['neural'][k]\n   497→    ax[3, 1].imshow(ev, aspect='auto', vmin=0, vmax=np.percentile(ev, 99.5),\n   498→                    extent=[x[0][0], x[0][-1], ev.shape[0], 0], cmap='gray_r')\n   499→    ax5 = ax[3, 1].twinx()\n   500→    ax5.plot(x[0], pos, 'r', lw=1)\n   501→    ax[3, 1].set_xlabel('time from trial start (s)')\n   502→    ax[3, 1].set_ylabel('cell')\n   503→    ax5.set_ylabel('position (cm)')\n   504→    fig.tight_layout()\n   505→    fig.savefig(os.path.join(plotdir, 'processing_%s_outputs.png' % sid), dpi=110)\n   506→    plt.close(fig)\n   507→\n   508→    # ---- per-trial summary: all trials of the session ----\n   509→    fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 4, figsize=(16, 5))\n   510→    trials = sess['kept_trials']\n   511→    ax[0].plot(trials, [rz_coords[i, 0] for i in trials], 'g.', label='zone start')\n   512→    ax[0].plot(trials, [rz_coords[i, 1] for i in trials], 'r.', label='zone end')\n   513→    ax[0].plot(trials, [np.nan if np.isnan(0) else 0 for i in trials], alpha=0)\n   514→    ax[0].set_xlabel('lap'); ax[0].set_ylabel('reward zone (cm)'); ax[0].legend(fontsize=7)\n   515→    ax[1].plot(trials, [o[5][0] for o in sess['output']], 'k.')\n   516→    ax[1].set_xlabel('lap'); ax[1].set_ylabel('reward outcome')\n   517→    ax[2].plot(trials, [x[3][0] for x in sess['input']], 'b.')\n   518→    ax[2].set_xlabel('lap'); ax[2].set_ylabel('previous outcome (input)')\n   519→    ax[3].plot(trials, [x[1][0] for x in sess['input']], 'm.')\n   520→    ax[3].set_xlabel('lap'); ax[3].set_ylabel('environment (input)')\n   521→    fig.suptitle('%s per-trial variables' % sid)\n   522→    fig.tight_layout()\n   523→    fig.savefig(os.path.join(plotdir, 'processing_%s_trials.png' % sid), dpi=110)\n   524→    plt.close(fig)\n   525→\n   526→\n   527→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   528→# main\n   529→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   530→def _worker(args):\n   531→    fn, show, plotdir = args\n   532→    try:\n   533→        return process_session(fn, show=show, plotdir=plotdir)\n   534→    except Exception:\n   535→        traceback.print_exc()\n   536→        return None, dict(file=os.path.basename(fn), error=True)\n   537→\n   538→\n   539→def main():\n   540→    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n   541→    ap.add_argument('outfile')\n   542→    ap.add_argument('--full', action='store_true', default=True)\n   543→    ap.add_argument('--sample', action='store_true')\n   544→    ap.add_argument('--show-processing', action='store_true')\n   545→    ap.add_argument('--nproc', type=int, default=8)\n   546→    args = ap.parse_args()\n   547→\n   548→    files = sorted(glob.glob('/app/data/sub-*/*.nwb'))\n   549→    if args.sample:\n   550→        # two sessions from two different subjects (smallest files, for speed)\n   551→        chosen, subs = [], set()\n   552→        for fn in sorted(files, key=os.path.getsize):\n   553→            sub = os.path.basename(fn).split('_')[0]\n   554→            if sub in subs:\n   555→                continue\n   556→            chosen.append(fn); subs.add(sub)\n   557→            if len(chosen) == 2:\n   558→                break\n   559→        files = sorted(chosen)\n   560→    print('processing %d sessions' % len(files))\n   561→\n   562→    show_files = set(files[:2]) if args.show_processing else set()\n   563→    t0 = time.time()\n   564→    results = []\n   565→    if args.nproc > 1 and len(files) > 1:\n   566→        import multiprocessing as mp\n   567→        ctx = mp.get_context('spawn')\n   568→        with ctx.Pool(min(args.nproc, len(files)), maxtasksperchild=1) as pool:\n   569→            for res in pool.imap(_worker, [(fn, fn in show_files, '/app') for fn in files]):\n   570→                results.append(res)\n   571→                st = res[1]\n   572→                print('[%5.1f s] %s: %d cells (%d iscell, %d interneurons), '\n   573→                      '%d/%d trials kept  (load %.1fs dff %.1fs)'\n   574→                      % (time.time() - t0, st.get('file'), st.get('n_neurons', -1),\n   575→                         st.get('n_iscell', -1), st.get('n_interneuron', -1),\n   576→                         st.get('n_trials_kept', -1), st.get('n_trials_raw', -1),\n   577→                         st.get('t_load', 0), st.get('t_dff', 0)))\n   578→    else:\n   579→        for fn in files:\n   580→            res = _worker((fn, fn in show_files, '/app'))\n   581→            results.append(res)\n   582→            st = res[1]\n   583→            print('[%5.1f s] %s: %d cells, %d/%d trials kept'\n   584→                  % (time.time() - t0, st.get('file'), st.get('n_neurons', -1),\n   585→                     st.get('n_trials_kept', -1), st.get('n_trials_raw', -1)))\n   586→\n   587→    sessions = [r[0] for r in results if r[0] is not None and len(r[0]['neural']) >= 2]\n   588→    stats = [r[1] for r in results]\n   589→    print('kept %d sessions with >= 2 trials' % len(sessions))\n   590→\n   591→    subjects = sorted({s['subject'] for s in sessions}, key=lambda x: int(x[1:]))\n   592→    brain_regions = ['CA1']\n   593→    data = dict(\n   594→        neural=[s['neural'] for s in sessions],\n   595→        input=[s['input'] for s in sessions],\n   596→        output=[s['output'] for s in sessions],\n   597→        subjects=subjects,\n   598→        subject_idx=np.array([subjects.index(s['subject']) for s in sessions], dtype=np.int64),\n   599→        brain_regions=brain_regions,\n   600→        brain_region_idx=[np.zeros(s['n_neurons'], dtype=np.int64) for s in sessions],\n   601→        input_names=INPUT_NAMES,\n   602→        output_names=OUTPUT_NAMES,\n   603→        output_values=OUTPUT_VALUES,\n   604→        metadata=dict(\n   605→            task_description=(\n   606→                'Head-fixed mice ran laps on a 450 cm virtual linear track for water '\n   607→                'reward delivered in a hidden 50 cm reward zone (location A: 80-130 cm, '\n   608→                'B: 200-250 cm, C: 320-370 cm). The reward zone was moved to a new '\n   609→                'location after 30 laps on switch days, and on one switch day the '\n   610→                'virtual environment also changed (ENV1 vs ENV2). Reward was omitted on '\n   611→                'a subset of laps. Two-photon calcium imaging of hippocampal CA1 '\n   612→                'pyramidal neurons (GCaMP7f). The decoder predicts, from CA1 '\n   613→                'deconvolved calcium activity: distance to the reward zone, absolute '\n   614→                'track position, running speed, licking, the active reward zone '\n   615→                'location and the trial reward outcome. (Sosa, Plitt & Giocomo 2025, '\n   616→                'Nature Neuroscience; DANDI 001361)'),\n   617→            time_bin_size=float(np.mean([s['dt'] for s in sessions]) * 1000.0),\n   618→            temporal_alignment_event='trial start (teleport into the linear track, position 0 cm)',\n   619→            off_start=-float(np.mean([s['dt'] for s in sessions])),\n   620→            off_end=None,\n   621→            neural_signal=('deconvolved calcium events (OASIS) computed from per-trial '\n   622→                           'maximin dF/F of neuropil-corrected suite2p ROI fluorescence, '\n   623→                           'exactly as in reward_relative.preprocessing.dff'),\n   624→            neuron_curation=('suite2p manual curation (iscell), exclusion of putative '\n   625→                             'interneurons with Pearson r > 0.5 between dF/F and running speed, '\n   626→                             'and exclusion of cells whose per-trial maximin baseline falls '\n   627→                             'below 5%% of their median raw fluorescence (dF/F numerically '\n   628→                             'undefined for these cells)'),\n   629→            trial_curation=('complete laps only ([trial_start-1, teleport-1) frames); the first '\n   630→                            'lap of each session is dropped because previous-trial outcome is '\n   631→                            'undefined; laps with lick-sensor errors (>30% of frames with '\n   632→                            'cumulative lick count > 2) are dropped'),\n   633→            deviations_from_reference=('the reference GLM/decoder analyses discard samples with '\n   634→                                       'speed < 2 cm/s; all within-trial samples are kept here '\n   635→                                       'because speed (including a < 2 cm/s class) is a decoder '\n   636→                                       'output and the decoder requires contiguous trial time series'),\n   637→            session_info=[dict(subject=s['subject'], session=s['session_id'], scene=s['scene'],\n   638→                               date=s['date'], n_neurons=s['n_neurons'],\n   639→                               n_trials=len(s['neural']),\n   640→                               laps=s['kept_trials'].tolist()) for s in sessions],\n   641→            source='DANDI dandiset 001361 (NWB), Sosa, Plitt & Giocomo 2025',\n   642→        ),\n   643→    )\n   644→\n   645→    # ------------------------- summary / sanity checks -------------------------\n   646→    ntr = sum(len(s['neural']) for s in sessions)\n   647→    nneu = sum(s['n_neurons'] for s in sessions)\n   648→    nfr = sum(int(a.shape[1]) for s in sessions for a in s['neural'])\n   649→    print('\\n=== conversion summary ===')\n   650→    print('sessions %d, subjects %d, trials %d, neurons %d, frames %d'\n   651→          % (len(sessions), len(subjects), ntr, nneu, nfr))\n   652→    ok = [s for s in stats if not s.get('error')]\n   653→    print('iscell total %d, interneurons excluded %d (%.2f%%), unstable-baseline cells '\n   654→          'excluded %d (%.3f%%), neurons kept %d'\n   655→          % (sum(s['n_iscell'] for s in ok), sum(s['n_interneuron'] for s in ok),\n   656→             100 * sum(s['n_interneuron'] for s in ok) / max(1, sum(s['n_iscell'] for s in ok)),\n   657→             sum(s['n_bad_baseline'] for s in ok),\n   658→             100 * sum(s['n_bad_baseline'] for s in ok) / max(1, sum(s['n_iscell'] for s in ok)),\n   659→             sum(s['n_neurons'] for s in ok)))\n   660→    print('trials raw %d, lick-error %d, kept %d'\n   661→          % (sum(s['n_trials_raw'] for s in ok), sum(s['n_lick_error'] for s in ok),\n   662→             sum(s['n_trials_kept'] for s in ok)))\n   663→    print('reward rate %.3f' % (sum(s['n_rewarded'] for s in ok) / max(1, sum(s['n_trials_raw'] for s in ok))))\n   664→    print('median reward-zone onset error %.2f cm (expect 0-15)'\n   665→          % from the reference code / paper methods\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# reward_relative.behavior.reward_zone_dict (X/Y/Z are the internal names of A/B/C)\nREWARD_ZONE_DICT = {'A': [80.0, 130.0], 'B': [200.0, 250.0], 'C': [320.0, 370.0],\n                    'T': [275.0, 325.0]}\nCHANGE_TRIAL = 30           # behavior.get_reward_zones(change_trial=30)\nNEU_COEF = 0.7              # utilities.default_dff_method['neu_coef']\nTAU = 0.7                   # suite2p ops['tau'] in the reference suite2p notebook\nBASELINE_SMOOTH_SIGMA = 15  # frames, preprocessing.dff maximin\nMAXIMIN_WINDOW = 300        # frames (~20 s), preprocessing.dff maximin\nDFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA = 2        # frames (~0.129 s), preprocessing.dff\nOASIS_BATCH = 2000          # preprocessing.dff\nINTERNEURON_R_THRESH = 0.5  # spatial.is_putative_interneuron(r_thresh=0.5)\n# extra numerical-stability rule (not in the reference): drop cells whose per-trial\n# maximin baseline falls below this fraction of the cell median raw fluorescence\nBASELINE_MIN_FRAC = 0.05\nLICK_ERROR_THRESH = 0.30    # Methods: >30% of frames with cumulative lick count > 2\nTRACK_LENGTH = 450.0        # cm\n\n# output discretisation edges (np.digitize, right=False unless noted)\nPOSITION_EDGES = [90.0, 180.0, 270.0, 360.0]\nSPEED_EDGES = [2.0, 10.0, 20.0, 40.0]\nRZ_LABELS = ['A', 'B', 'C']\n\nINPUT_NAMES = ['time_from_trial_start', 'environment', 'trial_number',\n               'previous_trial_outcome']\nOUTPUT_NAMES = ['reward_zone_distance', 'position', 'speed', 'lick',\n                'reward_zone_location', 'reward_outcome']\nOUTPUT_VALUES = [\n    ['< -50 cm', '-50 to ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# helpers copied / adapted from the reference code\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef nansmooth(a, sig, axis=-1):\n    \"\"\"reward_relative.utilities.nansmooth: gaussian smoothing that ignores NaNs.\"\"\"\n    nan_inds = np.isnan(a)\n    a_nanless = np.where(nan_inds, 0.0, a)\n    one = np.ones(a.shape)\n    one[nan_inds] = 0.001\n    a_nanless = gaussian_filter1d(a_nanless, sig, axis=axis)\n    one = gaussian_filter1d(one, sig, axis=axis)\n    return a_nanless / one\n\n\ndef scene_reward_zones(scene, n_trials):\n    \"\"\"Per-trial reward zone coordinates and labels from the VR scene name.\n\n    Mirrors reward_relative.behavior.get_reward_zones: a constant zone for\n    'Env<n>_Location<X>' scenes, and a switch after CHANGE_TRIAL trials for the\n    'Env<n>_Location<X>_to_<Y>' and 'Env<n>_<X>_to_Env<m>_<Y>' switch scenes.\n    \"\"\"\n    s = scene\n    if '_to_' not in s:\n        # e.g. Env1_LocationA / Env2_LocationC\n        label = s[-1]\n        labels = [label] * n_trials\n    else:\n        pre, post = s.split('_to_')\n        first = pre[-1]              # ...LocationX  or  Env1_X\n        second = post[-1]            # Y  or  Env2_Y\n        labels = [first] * min(CHANGE_TRIAL, n_trials)\n        if n_trials > CHANGE_TRIAL:\n            labels += [second] * (n_trials - CHANGE_TRIAL)\n    if any(l not in REWARD_ZONE_DICT for l in labels):\n        raise ValueError('unrecognised scene %s' % scene)\n    coords = np.array([REWARD_ZONE_DICT[l] for l in labels], dtype=float)\n    return coords, np.array(labels)\n\n\ndef compute_dff_and_events(F, Fneu, starts, stops, fs):\n    \"\"\"Reference reward_relative.preprocessing.dff with\n    neuropil_method='subtract', baseline_method='maximin', subtract_baseline=True,\n    keep_teleports=False, deconvolve=True.\n\n    F, Fneu: (n_cells, n_frames) raw suite2p traces\n    starts, stops: trial_start / teleport frame indices\n    fs: frames per second per plane\n    Returns dff, events (both NaN outside of trials).\n    \"\"\"\n    f_ = np.full(F.shape, np.nan)\n    fneu_ = np.full(F.shape, np.nan)\n    for start, stop in zip(starts, stops):\n        f_[:, start - 1:stop - 1] = F[:, start - 1:stop - 1]\n        fneu_[:, start - 1:stop - 1] = Fneu[:, start - 1:stop - 1]\n\n    nanmask = ~np.isnan(f_[0, :])\n\n    # neuropil correction\n    f_ -= NEU_COEF * fneu_\n\n    flow = np.full(F.shape, np.nan)\n    events = np.full(F.shape, np.nan)\n\n    for start, stop in zip(starts, stops):\n        sl = slice(start - 1, stop - 1)\n        # add back the per-trial neuropil mean so dF/F is not divided by small numbers\n        f_[:, sl] = f_[:, sl] + NEU_COEF * np.nanmean(fneu_[:, sl], axis=1, keepdims=True)\n        # maximin baseline within the trial\n        base = gaussian_filter1d(f_[:, sl], BASELINE_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=-1)\n        base = minimum_filter1d(base, MAXIMIN_WINDOW, axis=-1)\n        base = maximum_filter1d(base, MAXIMIN_WINDOW, axis=-1)\n        flow[:, sl] = base\n\n    dff = np.full(F.shape, np.nan)\n    dff[:, nanmask] = (f_[:, nanmask] - flow[:, nanmask]) / np.abs(flow[:, nanmask])\n\n    # Cells whose maximin baseline collapses towards zero (this happens when the\n    # neuropil trace is much larger than the ROI trace, so the neuropil-corrected\n    # signal crosses zero) give a numerically meaningless dF/F, because dF/F divides\n    # by |baseline|. Flag them so they can be excluded from the converted data.\n    with np.errstate(invalid='ignore'):\n        min_base = np.nanmin(flow, axis=1)\n        scale = np.nanmedian(np.where(np.isnan(flow), np.nan, F), axis=1)\n    bad_baseline = ~(min_base > BASELINE_MIN_FRAC * scale)\n\n    for start, stop in zip(starts, stops):\n        sl = slice(start - 1, stop - 1)\n        dff[:, sl] = nansmooth(dff[:, sl], DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n        events[:, sl] = dcnv.oasis(\n            np.ascontiguousarray(dff[:, sl], dtype=np.float32), OASIS_BATCH, TAU, fs)\n\n    return dff, events, bad_baseline\n\n\ndef reward_zone_distance(pos, rz_start, rz_end):\n    \"\"\"Signed distance (cm) to the nearest point of the reward zone.\n\n    0 anywhere inside the zone, negative before the zone, positive after it.\n    \"\"\"\n    d = np.zeros_like(pos)\n    before = pos < rz_start\n    after = pos > rz_end\n    d[before] = pos[before] - rz_start\n    d[after] = pos[after] - rz_end\n    return d\n\n\ndef digitize_rz_distance(d):\n    \"\"\"7-way discretisation of the reward-zone distance (see task description).\"\"\"\n    out = np.full(d.shape, 3, dtype=np.int64)   # exactly 0 -> in the zone\n    out[d < -50.0] = 0\n    out[(d >= -50.0) & (d < -10.0)] = 1\n    out[(d >= -10.0) & (d < 0.0)] = 2\n    out[(d > 0.0) & (d <= 10.0)] = 4\n    out[(d > 10.0) & (d <= 50.0)] = 5\n    out[d > 50.0] = 6\n    return out\n\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# per-session conversion\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef load_session(fn):\n    \"\"\"Read everything needed from one NWB file (h5py, no pynwb needed).\"\"\"\n    with h5py.File(fn, 'r') as f:\n        beh = f['processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries']\n        g = lambda k: beh[k]['data'][()].astype(np.float64)\n        d = dict(\n            file=fn,\n            subject=f['general/subject/subject_id'][()].decode(),\n            session_id=f['general/session_id'][()].decode(),\n            scene=f['identifier'][()].decode().split('/')[-1],\n            date=f['identifier'][()].decode().split('/')[-2],\n            imaging_rate=float(f['general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/imaging_rate'][()]),\n            location=f['general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/location'][()].decode(),\n            pos=g('position'), speed=g('speed'), lick=g('lick'),\n            rzone_flag=g('reward_zone'), env=g('environment'),\n            trialnum=g('trial number'), autoreward=g('autoreward'),\n            scanning=g('scanning'),\n            t=beh['position']['timestamps'][()].astype(np.float64),\n            reward_t=beh['Reward']['timestamps'][()].astype(np.float64),\n            starts=np.where(g('trial_start') > 0)[0],\n            stops=np.where(g('teleport') > 0)[0],\n        )\n        ps = f['processing/ophys/ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation']\n        iscell = ps['iscell'][()][:, 0].astype(bool)\n        planes = sorted(int(p) for p in np.unique(ps['planeIdx'][()]))\n        Fl, Nl = [], []\n        for p in planes:\n            rois = f['processing/ophys/Fluorescence/plane%d/rois' % p][()]\n            keep = iscell[rois]\n            Fl.append(f['processing/ophys/Fluorescence/plane%d/data' % p][:, keep].T)\n            Nl.append(f['processing/ophys/Neuropil/plane%d/data' % p][:, keep].T)\n        d['F'] = np.concatenate(Fl, axis=0).astype(np.float64)\n        d['Fneu'] = np.concatenate(Nl, axis=0).astype(np.float64)\n        # In 10 sessions (m17, m18) the ophys series contain exactly one frame more\n        # than the behavioural series. Both streams start at t = 0 (ophys\n        # starting_time = 0 and behaviour timestamps[0] = 0), so the extra imaging\n        # frame is at the end: truncate to the common length so that frame i of the\n        # neural data corresponds to frame i of the behaviour.\n        n_beh = d['pos'].shape[0]\n        if d['F'].shape[1] != n_beh:\n            print('  NOTE %s: ophys has %d frames, behaviour %d; truncating to %d'\n                  % (os.path.basename(fn), d['F'].shape[1], n_beh, min(d['F'].shape[1], n_beh)))\n            n = min(d['F'].shape[1], n_beh)\n            d['F'] = d['F'][:, :n]\n            d['Fneu'] = d['Fneu'][:, :n]\n            for k in ['pos', 'speed', 'lick', 'rzone_flag', 'env', 'trialnum',\n                      'autoreward', 'scanning', 't']:\n                d[k] = d[k][:n]\n            d['starts'] = d['starts'][d['starts'] < n - 1]\n            d['stops'] = d['stops'][:len(d['starts'])]\n            d['starts'] = d['starts'][:len(d['stops'])]\n        d['n_planes'] = len(planes)\n        d['n_iscell'] = int(iscell.sum())\n        d['n_roi_total'] = int(iscell.size)\n    return d\n\n\ndef process_session(fn, show=False, plotdir='/app'):\n    \"\"\"Convert one NWB session. Returns (session_dict, stats_dict).\"\"\"\n    t0 = time.time()\n    S = load_session(fn)\n    t_load = time.time() - t0\n\n    starts, stops = S['starts'], S['stops']\n    n_trials = len(starts)\n    assert n_trials == len(stops) and np.all(starts < stops) and starts[0] >= 1\n\n    fs = S['imaging_rate'] / S['n_planes']\n    dt = float(np.median(np.diff(S['t'])))\n\n    # ---- neural: dF/F + OASIS events (reference preprocessing.dff) ----\n    t1 = time.time()\n    dff, events, bad_baseline = compute_dff_and_events(S['F'], S['Fneu'], starts, stops, fs)\n    t_dff = time.time() - t1\n\n    # ---- cell curation: putative interneurons (dF/F vs speed r > 0.5) ----\n    t2 = time.time()\n    valid = ~np.isnan(dff[0, :])\n    sp = S['speed'][valid]\n    X = dff[:, valid]\n    Xc = X - X.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    spc = sp - sp.mean()\n    denom = np.sqrt((Xc ** 2).sum(axis=1) * (spc ** 2).sum())\n    speed_corr = (Xc @ spc) / np.where(denom == 0, np.nan, denom)\n    is_int = np.nan_to_num(speed_corr, nan=0.0) > INTERNEURON_R_THRESH\n    keep_cells = (~is_int) & (~bad_baseline)\n    t_int = time.time() - t2\n\n    # ---- trial-level task variables ----\n    rz_coords, np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=np.int64)\n    lick_error = np.zeros(n_trials, dtype=bool)\n    rz_onset_pos = np.full(n_trials, np.nan)\n    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(zip(starts, stops)):\n        sl = slice(s - 1, e - 1)\n        rzflag = S['rzone_flag'][sl] > 0\n        got_reward = np.any((reward_frames >= s - 1) & (reward_frames < e - 1))\n        # behavior.get_trial_types: reward delivered AND reward zone active\n        isreward[i] = int(got_reward and np.any(rzflag))\n        env_trial[i] = int(np.round(np.median(S['env'][sl])))\n        L = S['lick'][sl]\n        lick_error[i] = (np.sum(L > 2) / len(L)) > LICK_ERROR_THRESH\n        if np.any(rzflag):\n            rz_onset_pos[i] = S['pos'][sl][np.argmax(rzflag)]\n\n    # sanity check: scene-derived zone vs zone entry position measured in the data\n    obs = rz_onset_pos[~np.isnan(rz_onset_pos)]\n    exp = rz_coords[~np.isnan(rz_onset_pos), 0]\n    zone_err = float(np.nanmedian(obs - exp)) if len(obs) else np.nan\n    if len(obs) and not (-1.0 <= zone_err <= 15.0):\n        print('  WARNING %s: reward-zone mismatch, median(onset-zone_start)=%.1f cm'\n              % (os.path.basename(fn), zone_err))\n\n    # ---- build per-trial arrays ----\n    neural, inp, out = [], [], []\n    kept_trials = []\n    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(zip(starts, stops)):\n        if i == 0:\n            continue                      # previous-trial outcome undefined\n        if lick_error[i]:\n            continue                      # invalid lick data (reference sets to NaN)\n        sl = slice(s - 1, e - 1)\n        T = (e - 1) - (s - 1)\n        ev = events[np.ix_(keep_cells, np.arange(s - 1, e - 1))]\n        if not np.all(np.isfinite(ev)):\n            print('  WARNING %s trial %d: non-finite events, trial dropped'\n                  % (os.path.basename(fn), i))\n            continue\n\n        pos = S['pos'][sl]\n        speed = S['speed'][sl]\n        lick = S['lick'][sl]\n        tt = S['t'][sl] - S['t'][s]       # time from trial start (s)\n\n        x = np.empty((4, T), dtype=np.float32)\n        x[0] = tt\n        x[1] = env_trial[i]\n        x[2] = i                           # lap index within the session\n        x[3] = isreward[i - 1]             # previous trial outcome\n\n        d_rz = reward_zone_distance(pos, rz_coords[i, 0], rz_coords[i, 1])\n        y = np.empty((6, T), dtype=np.int64)\n        y[0] = digitize_rz_distance(d_rz)\n        y[1] = np.digitize(pos, POSITION_EDGES)\n        y[2] = np.digitize(speed, SPEED_EDGES)\n        y[3] = (lick >= 1).astype(np.int64)\n        y[4] = RZ_LABELS.index(rz_labels[i])\n        y[5] = isreward[i]\n\n        neural.append(ev.astype(np.float32))\n        inp.append(x)\n        out.append(y)\n        kept_trials.append(i)\n\n    sess = dict(\n        neural=neural, input=inp, output=out,\n        subject=S['subject'], session_id=S['session_id'], scene=S['scene'],\n        date=S['date'], location=S['location'],\n        n_neurons=int(keep_cells.sum()), kept_trials=np.array(kept_trials),\n        dt=dt,\n    )\n    stats = dict(\n        file=os.path.basename(fn), subject=S['subject'], n_neurons=int(keep_cells.sum()),\n        n_trials_raw=n_trials, n_trials_kept=len(neural),\n        n_lick_error=int(lick_error.sum()), n_rewarded=int(isreward.sum()),\n ev_max=float(np.nanmax(events[keep_cells])), dt=dt,\n        t_load=t_load, t_dff=t_dff, t_int=t_int, t_total=time.time() - t0,\n    )\n\n    if show:\n        try:\n            make_processing_plots(S, dff, events, keep_cells, rz_coords, rz_labels,\n                                 isreward, sess, plotdir)\n        except Exception:\n            traceback.print_exc()\n\n    return sess, stats\n\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# visualisation of every processing step (--show-processing)\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndef make_processing_plots(S, dff, events, keep_cells, rz_coords, rz_labels,\n                          isreward, sess, plotdir):\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use('Agg')\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    sid = '%s_ses-%s' % (S['subject'], S['session_id'])\n    starts, stops = S['starts'], ax[1].set_ylabel('neuropil F')\n    for c in cells:\n        ax[2].plot(tt, dff[c, a:b], lw=0.7)\n    ax[2].set_ylabel('dF/F')\n    for c in cells:\n        ax[3].plot(tt, events[c, a:b], lw=0.7)\n    ax[3].set_ylabel('events (OASIS)')\n    ax[4].plot(tt, S['pos'][a:b], 'k', lw=1)\n    for i in range(i0, i1):\n        s, e = starts[i] - 1, stops[i] - 1\n        ax[4].axhspan(rz_coords[i, 0],  ax[5].axhline(2, color='gray', ls=':')\n    ax[5].set_ylabel('speed (cm/s)')\n    ax[6].plot(tt, S['lick'][a:b], 'm', lw=1, label='cumulative lick count')\n    rf = np.searchsorted(S['t'], I will follow the reference: compute dF/F from NWB `Fluorescence` + `Neuropil` and deconvolve with OASIS (suite2p dcnv), matching `preprocessing.dff`.\n    45→- Cell curation: suite2p manual curation is stored in NWB as `PlaneSegmentation/iscell[:,0]`; `general/subject/subject_id` = mouse; `general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane`:\n    64→  location `hippocampus, CA1`, indicator GCaMP7f, imaging_rate 15.5078125 Hz (31.015625 for 2-plane mice m17, m18).\n    65→- `processing/behavior/BehavioralTimeSeries/` (all 1-D, one sample per imaging frame, shared timestamps, dt=0.064484 s):\n    66→  `position` (cm; -500 before VR sync, -50 during teleport/ITI), `speed` (cm/s, smoothed),\n    67→  `lick` (cumulative lick count per frame, 0-7), `reward_zone` (cumulative reward-zone-entry flag per frame),\n    68→  `environment` (0 = ENV1, 1 = ENV2, -1 before sync), `trial number` (0-indexed lap, -1 before sync),\n    69→  `trial_start` (binary), `teleport` (binary), `autoreward`, `scanning` (1 = scanning, -1 before sync).\n    70→  `Reward` is a sparse TimeSeries: one sample per delivered reward (data = 0.004 mL) with timestamps that\n    71→  exactly equal frame timestamps (verified on all 152 sessions).\n    72→- `processing/ophys/`: `Fluorescence/plane<i>/data` (frames x ROIs, raw F), `Neuropil/plane<i>/data` (Fneu),\n    73→  `Deconvolved/plane<i>/data` (suite2p deconvolution of raw F, NOT the paper events),\n    74→  `ImageSegmentation/PlaneSegmentation` with `iscell` (n_roi x 2: manual-curation flag + probability) and `planeIdx`.\n    75→  2-plane mice (m17, m18) have plane0 and plane1 groups; the ROI table is concatenated over planes (planeIdx 0/1).\n    76→  **No dF/F is stored**, so it must be computed as in the reference `preprocessing.dff`.\n    77→\n    78→### Dataset Size (from data files)\n    79→| Statistic | Value |\n    80→|-----------|-------|\n    81→| Neurons (total, iscell==1, all sessions) | 138,678 |\n    82→| Neurons / session | mean 912.4, min 155, max 2341 |\n    83→| Subjects | 11 |\n    84→| Sessions / subject | 14 for all except m11 (12); n = 81 out of 12,376 trials removed across 11 switch mice |\n   112→| Mice beginning in ENV1 / ENV2 | 9 / 2 (m17, m18 began in ENV 2) | Most mice began the task in ENV 1 (n = 9 mice; two mice (m17 and m18) began in ENV 2) |\n   113→| Sessions / subject | 14 days; m11 imaged from day 3 | The task was imaged starting from day 1 for all mice except m11, for whom imaging started on day 3 |\n   114→| Trials / session | 80-100 targeted; mean +/- s.d. 80.5 +/- 7.4 (across 14 mice) | We targeted 80-100 trials per session ... (mean +/- s.d., 80.5 +/- 7.4 trials across 14 mice, all imaging days) |\n   115→| Trials (total, imaged, 11 switch mice) | 12,376 | ~0.65% of all imaged trials, n = 81 out of 12,376 trials removed across 11 switch mice |\n   116→| Neurons / session | 155-2172 putative pyramidal neurons | This approach yielded 155-2172 putative pyramidal neurons per session |\n   117→| Neural data time bin | 0.0645 s (~15.5 Hz imaging frame) | All behavioral and neural time series were sampled at ~15.5 Hz, the imaging frame rate |\n   118→| Behavior data time bin | same 0.0645 s (VR aligned to imaging frames) | as above |\n   119→| Track length | 450 cm | linear track position (from 0 to 450 cm) |\n   120→| Reward zone width / locations | 50 cm; A=[80,130], B=[200,250], C=[320,370] | reward_zone_dict in behavior.py (X/Y/Z map to A/B/C) |\n   121→| Switch trial | after 30 trials | Each switch occurred after 30 trials |\n   122→| Autoreward | first 10 trials of a new condition | On the first ten trials of any new condition ... the reward was automatically delivered at the end of the zone if the mouse had not yet licked |\n   123→| Lick-error trials removed | 81 of 12,376 (~0.65%) | detected by >30% of the 0.0645 s imaging frame samples in the trial containing a cumulative lick count >2 |\n   124→| Putative interneurons excluded | 0.42 +/- 0.85% of cells (dF/F vs speed Pearson r > 0.5) | Additional putative interneurons were detected for exclusion ... by a Pearson correlation of >0.5 between their dF/F timeseries and the running speed |\n   125→| Speed threshold in paper analyses | 2 cm/s | deconvolved calcium event timeseries (at speeds of >2 cm s-1) |\n   126→| Reward omission rate | ~15% (measured in data: 15.3%) | omission trials analysed throughout Fig. 6 |\n   127→\n   128→My measured values (Step 2) agree: 152 sessions, 11 mice, 12,216 trial_start events, 80.37 +/- 6.16 trials/session,\n   129→155-2341 cells/session, and **exactly 81 trials** flagged by the paper lick-error rule (>30% of frames with\n   130→cumulative lick count > 2), a strong sanity check that loading and trial segmentation match the reference.\n   131→The paper 12,376 trials is 160 more than the 12,216 trial_start events in the NWB files (1.3% difference); the\n   132→NWB conversion appears to drop a few incomplete laps (e.g. a final lap without a teleport). Every NWB session\n   133→has exactly matching numbers of trial_start and teleport events, so all 12,216 complete laps are used.\n   134→The max of 2341 cells/session (m18 day 3, 2-plane) slightly exceeds the paper range 155-2172; this is the pooled\n   135→two-plane count and the paper range may have been computed per plane or on a subset of days.\n   136→\n   137→### Processing Details\n   138→- **Temporal alignment**: VR behaviour is already interpolated onto imaging frame times (vr_align_to_2P in the\n   139→  authors TwoPUtils repo); in the NWB files every behavioural series shares the imaging-frame timestamps.\n   140→- **Trial window**: reference code (preprocessing.dff, glmUtils.get_timeseries_data) uses\n   141→  [trial_start-1, teleport-1), i.e. from one frame before the trial-start flag up to one frame before teleport.\n   142→- **dF/F**: per-trial maximin baseline: neuropil subtraction (0.7 x Fneu, per-trial neuropil mean added back),\n   143→  Gaussian smooth (sigma = 15 frames), minimum filter (300 frames ~ 20 s), maximum filter (300 frames),\n   144→  dF/F = (F - baseline)/|baseline|, then Gaussian smoothing with sigma = 2 frames (~0.129 s).\n   145→- **Events**: OASIS deconvolution of smoothed dF/F per trial (suite2p.extraction.dcnv.oasis, batch 2000,\n   146→  tau = 0.7 s from the suite2p ops in the repo notebook, fs = frame_rate/n_planes = 15.5078 Hz).\n   147→- **Reward-relative position**: distance from the start of the active reward zone (rzone[i,0]) per trial.\n   148→- **GLM/decoder variables**: position (0-450 cm), RR position, rewarded binary (0 until reward delivery then 1\n   149→  until end of trial), speed, acceleration, smoothed binary licks.\n   150→\n   151→### Curation Steps\n   152→**Neuron curation rules**:\n   153→1. suite2p manual curation: keep ROIs with iscell[:,0] == 1 (the manual curation of the Methods).\n   154→2. Exclude putative interneurons: Pearson r > 0.5 between the cell dF/F and running speed (within-trial samples).\n   155→3. Planes are pooled for multi-plane mice (m17, m18).\n   156→\n   157→**Trial curation rules**:\n   158→1. Only complete laps, defined by paired trial_start/teleport flags; samples restricted to [start-1, stop-1).\n   159→2. Lick-sensor error trials (>30% of frames with cumulative lick count > 2, n = 81) have lick values set to NaN in\n   160→   the reference; because lick is a required decoder output here, these trials cannot provide a valid lick label,\n   161→   so they are dropped entirely (see Step 5).\n   162→\n   163→### Decoders Trained (paper)\n   164→| Decoded variable | Accuracy |\n   165→| Reward-relative (circular) position from deconvolved events of RR / track / non-RR remapping cells (Fig. 3) | reported as a mean cosine decode score (1 = perfect, 0 = chance) and as a z-score versus a 100-shuffle null, not as % classification accuracy. Fig. 3a shows a single fold of an example session; group means are well above shuffle. No classification accuracies are reported in the paper, so no direct numeric comparison with this task accuracy is possible. |\n   166→\n   167→---\n   168→\n   169→## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n   170→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   171→\n   172→I cross-checked the reference code, the NWB data, and the paper text. All within-trial samples are kept (documented deviation). |\n   185→| Interneuron exclusion | `spatial.is_putative_interneuron` (dF/F vs speed r > 0.5) | 0.6-3.6% per session in spot checks | 0.42 +/- |\n   208→| `general/subject/subject_id` | subjects / subject_idx | m3..m19 | - | 11 subjects |\n   209→| `general/optophysiology/ImagingPlane/location` | brain_regions | all cells CA1 | - | single region |\n   210→\n   211→### Key Decisions\n   212→1. **Neural signal = deconvolved events recomputed from F/Fneu** (not the stored `Deconvolved` series): the paper and\n   213→   all reference analyses (GLM, Fig. 3 decoder) use OASIS events derived from the per-trial maximin dF/F. The stored\n   214→   NWB `Deconvolved` series is suite2p deconvolution of raw F (no neuropil correction, no per-trial baseline), so it\n   215→   would not match the reference processing.\n   216→2. **Time bin = one imaging frame (64.4836 ms)**, no re-binning: the reference pipeline processes all neural and\n   217→   behavioural data at the imaging frame rate (~15.5 Hz) and the NWB behavioural series are already interpolated\n   218→   onto those frame times, so this preserves exact temporal alignment with no resampling.\n   219→3. **Trial window `[trial_start-1, teleport-1)`** exactly as in `preprocessing.dff` and\n   220→   `glmUtils.get_timeseries_data`; alignment event = trial start (entry to the linear track), off_start = -0.0645 s\n   221→   (the window begins one frame before the trial-start flag), off_end = None (variable trial length).\n   222→4. **Cell curation**: iscell==1 (suite2p manual curation) and exclusion of putative interneurons by dF/F-speed\n   223→   Pearson r > 0.5, both as in the reference. Planes pooled for m17/m18 (paper pools planes).\n   224→5. **No speed threshold**: the reference drops samples with speed < 2 cm/s for its GLM/decoder, but speed is a\n   225→   required decoder output here (with an explicit <2 cm/s class) and the decoder needs contiguous trial time series,\n   226→   so all within-trial samples are kept. This is a required deviation, documented here.\n   227→6. **Dropped trials**: (a) the 81 lick-sensor-error trials (>30% of frames with cumulative lick count > 2), because\n   228→   lick is a required output and the reference treats these lick values as invalid (NaN); (b) the first lap of each\n   229→   session (152 trials), because previous-trial outcome is a required input and is undefined for it (the preceding\n   230→   warm-up laps are not in the imaging record). Remaining trials: ~11,980 of 12,216 (98.1%).\n   231→7. **Per-trial variables are stored as constant time series** (shape (d, T)) so that every trial has a uniform\n   232→   (d_input, T) / (d_output, T) layout, and the instruction to make outputs time-varying where possible is honoured.\n   233→8. **Reward zone per trial from the scene name with the switch at lap 30** (reference `behavior.get_reward_zones`),\n   234→   verified against the data-derived reward-zone onset position in every session.\n   235→\n   236→### Planned Sanity Checks\n   237→- [x] Trial counts: 12,216 complete laps, 80.37 +/- 6.16 per session (paper: 80.5 +/- 7.4), 11 mice, 152 sessions.\n   238→- [x] Lick-error rule reproduces the paper 81 trials exactly.\n   239→- [x] Cells/session 155-2341 (paper 155-2172) and 138,678 total before interneuron exclusion.\n   240→- [ ] Scene-derived reward zone matches the data-derived reward-zone onset position in every session (per-session assert).\n   241→- [ ] Fraction of cells excluded as putative interneurons ~0.4-1% (paper 0.42 +/- 0.85%).\n   242→- [ ] Reward outcome fraction ~84.7% rewarded / 15.3% omission.\n   243→- [ ] Environment: 9 mice start in ENV1, 2 (m17, m18) in ENV2; environment flips at lap 30 only on cross-env days.\n   244→- [ ] Output distributions: position bins roughly uniform (~20% each); distance-to-zone bin 3 (inside zone) ~11%\n   245→  (50 cm of the 450-cm track); speed mostly in 10-40 cm/s bins; licks on a small fraction of frames.\n   246→- [ ] Spot-check raw-data sanity checks with np.allclose on neural, input and output values (Step 10).\n   247→- [ ] dF/F values in a plausible range (about -1 to 10) and events non-negative.\n   248→\n   249→---\n   250→\n   251→## Step 6: Script Development\n   252→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   253→\n   254→`/app/convert_data.py` implements the conversion. Structure:\n   255→- `load_session(fn)`: reads one NWB file with h5py: behavioural series, frame timestamps, sparse Reward\n   256→  timestamps, trial_start/teleport frame indices, scene name from `identifier`, and the iscell-filtered\n   257→  F / Fneu matrices of every imaging plane (concatenated over planes).\n   258→- `compute_dff_and_events(...)`: port of `reward_relative.preprocessing.dff` with the reference settings\n   259→  (neuropil subtract 0.7 with per-trial neuropil mean added back, per-trial maximin baseline:\n   260→  gaussian sigma=15 frames, minimum then maximum filter of 300 frames (~20 s), dF/F = (F-base)/|base|,\n   261→  gaussian sigma=2 frames), then per-trial OASIS deconvolution (`suite2p.extraction.dcnv.oasis`,\n   262→  batch 2000, tau 0.7, fs = imaging_rate/n_planes).\n   263→- `nansmooth`: port of `reward_relative.utilities.nansmooth`.\n   264→- interneuron exclusion: vectorised Pearson correlation between each cell dF/F and speed over the\n   265→  within-trial samples, threshold 0.5 (port of `spatial.is_putative_interneuron`, method=speed).\n   266→- `scene_reward_zones(scene, n_trials)`: port of `behavior.get_reward_zones` (dictionary A/B/C zones,\n   267→  switch after 30 laps, handles both `Env1_LocationA_to_C` and `Env1_C_to_Env2_B` scene styles).\n   268→- trial-type computation mirrors `behavior.get_trial_types` (rewarded = reward delivered AND reward-zone\n   269→  flag active) and the Methods lick-sensor-error rule (>30% of frames with cumulative lick count > 2).\n   270→- `reward_zone_distance` / `digitize_rz_distance` implement the required output discretisation.\n   271→- `--show-processing` writes three figures per session: (1) raw F, neuropil F, dF/F, events, position with\n   272→  the reward zone shaded, speed and licks/rewards over four consecutive laps with trial start/teleport\n   273→  lines, so temporal alignment of every stream is visible; (2) per-trial comparisons of each continuous\n   274→  behavioural variable with its discretised output plus session-wide value-vs-bin scatter plots (proving\n   275→  the discretisation) and the converted neural raster next to position; (3) per-lap reward zone, reward\n   276→  outcome, previous outcome and environment.\n   277→- Sessions are processed in parallel (`multiprocessing`, default 8 workers, `maxtasksperchild=1` to keep\n   278→  memory bounded); each worker returns only the converted per-trial arrays.\n   279→\n   280→Code inefficiencies identified: naive per-cell correlation loop for interneuron detection; float64\n   281→intermediates for F/Fneu.\n   282→Code speedups added: vectorised correlation (matrix-vector product), OASIS on float32 arrays, reading only\n   283→iscell columns from HDF5, parallel sessions.\n   284→\n   285→---\n   286→\n   287→## Step 7: Sample Conversion and Validation\n   288→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   289→\n   290→Command: `python -u 384→## Step 9: Full Conversion and Validation\n   385→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   386→\n   387→Commands:\n   388→`python -u /app/convert_data.py /app/converted_data.pkl --full --nproc 12` (199 s wall clock, 12 workers)\n   389→`python -u /app/train_decoder.py /app/converted_data.pkl --verify-only`\n   390→\n   391→### Output Files\n   392→- `converted_data.pkl`: 9.47 GB\n   393→- `conversion_full_out.txt`, `verification_full_out.txt`, `converted_data_session_stats.csv` (per-session stats)\n   394→\n   395→### Issue found and fixed during the first full run\n   396→The first full run crashed on 10 sessions of the two 2-plane mice (m17 x2, m18 x8): in those files the ophys\n   397→arrays have exactly one frame more than the behavioural series (e.g. 19,821 vs 19,820). Both streams start at\n   398→t = 0, so the extra imaging frame is at the end; `load_session` now truncates all streams to the common length\n   399→(and prints a NOTE). A second issue was found in the same run: in 8 sessions (again only m17/m18) dF/F reached\n   400→|dF/F| ~ 3000 for a handful of cells. Diagnosis: for those cells the neuropil trace is much larger than the ROI\n   401→trace (e.g. Fneu 4960 vs F 1487), so after the reference neuropil subtraction the corrected trace crosses zero\n   402→and the per-trial maximin baseline collapses to ~0; dF/F = (F - base)/|base| then explodes. Because the decoder\n   403→PCA would be dominated by such numerically degenerate cells, cells whose minimum within-trial baseline is below\n   404→5% of their median raw fluorescence are now excluded (25 cells = 0.018% of all curated cells). After the fix the\n   405→dF/F range over kept cells is [-16.0, 37.2] and the maximum deconvolved event is 7.1.\n   406→\n   407→### Consistency Check\n   408→| Statistic | Reference Paper | Reference Code | Reference Data (NWB) | Converted Data | Match? |\n   409→|-----------|-----------------|----------------|----------------|----------------|--------|\n   410→| Subjects | 11 switch mice | anim lists include mice not in DANDI | 11 | 11 | yes |\n   411→| Sessions | 14 days/mouse, m11 from day 3 | sessions_dict: 14 days/mouse | 152 | 152 | yes |\n   412→| Sessions per subject | 14 (m11: 12) | 14 | 14 (m11: 12) | 14 (m11: 12) | yes |\n   413→| Total neurons (curated) | 155-2172 per session | iscell | 138,678 iscell | 138,244 after interneuron (409) and unstable-baseline (25) exclusion | yes |\n   414→| Mean neurons/session | - | - | 912.4 | 909.5 (min 154, max 2319) | yes |\n   415→| Trials (total) | 12,376 imaged | - | 12,216 complete laps | 11,983 (12,216 - 81 lick-error - 152 first laps) | expected |\n   416→| Trials/session (mean) | 80.5 +/- 7.4 | - | 80.37 +/- 6.16 | 78.8 (min 39, max 99) | yes |\n   417→| Lick-error trials | 81 (0.65%) | rule with thr 0.35/0.5 | 81 at the paper thr 0.30 | 81 dropped | exact |\n   418→| Rewarded trials | omissions ~15% | isreward rule | 84.7% | 84.3% of frames rewarded; The only messages in `/app/conversion_full_out.txt` are the informational NOTE\n   439→lines for the 10 m17/m18 sessions where the ophys stream is one frame longer than the behavioural stream\n   440→(handled by truncation, see Step 9).\n   441→\n   442→### Check 2: Independent sanity checks (`/app/cache/sanity_checks.py`)\n   443→These checks load the raw NWB files directly and re-derive quantities with code written independently of\n   444→`convert_data.py`, then compare with `np.allclose`. Three sessions were chosen to cover the different session\n   445→types: `m11 ses-03` (1 plane, within-environment reward-zone switch B->A), `m3 ses-08` (cross-environment\n   446→switch `Env1_C_to_Env2_B`) and `m18 ses-13` (2-plane mouse, one of the sessions with the frame-count offset).\n   447→All 39 checks passed (`0 checks failed`):\n   448→- **neural**: an independent re-implementation of the reference dF/F (neuropil subtraction, per-trial maximin\n   449→  baseline, sigma=2 smoothing) + OASIS deconvolution, plus the two curation rules, reproduces the number of\n   450→  kept cells exactly (154/154, 778/778, 1427/1427) and the stored event matrices for the first, middle and last\n   451→  trial of each session (`np.allclose`, atol 1e-5). A single-value spot check\n   452→  (`neural[trial 5, neuron 3, timepoint 10]`) also matches, e.g. 0.022363 for m11 ses-03.\n   453→- **input**: `time_from_trial_start` equals `timestamps[start-1:stop-1] - timestamps[start]` for the first,\n   454→  middle and last trial; `environment` equals the NWB environment value of the lap; `trial_number` equals the\n   455→  NWB lap index; `previous_trial_outcome` equals the independently recomputed `isreward` of the preceding lap\n   456→  for every trial of the session.\n   457→- **output**: every one of the six outputs was recomputed for every kept trial from raw NWB position/speed/lick\n   458→  and from the scene-derived reward zone, and matched exactly (reward-zone distance bins, position bins, speed\n   459→  bins, lick binary, reward-zone location, reward outcome).\n   460→- **structure**: `subject_idx` matches the per-session subject, `brain_region_idx` lengths match the neuron\n   461→  counts, and sampled neural arrays contain no NaN/Inf.\n   462→\n   463→### Check 3: Reference code comparison\n   464→| Step | Reference | This conversion | Same? |\n   465→|------|-----------|-----------------|-------|\n   466→| (a) data loading | `TwoPUtils.sess` + `vr_align_to_2P` build `sess.vr_data` (per imaging frame) and `sess.timeseries` from suite2p F/Fneu | NWB behavioural series are exactly that aligned product; F/Fneu read from `processing/ophys` | yes (the NWB files are the published form of `sess`) |\n   467→| (b) neuron filtering | `iscell` from suite2p manual curation; `spatial.is_putative_interneuron(ts_key='dff', method='speed', r_thresh=0.5)` | identical rules (iscell + dF/F-speed r > 0.5); plus an extra numerical-stability rule (25 cells, 0.018%) for cells whose maximin baseline collapses to ~0 | yes + documented addition |\n   468→| (c) temporal alignment | trial window `[trial_start-1, teleport-1)` in `preprocessing.dff` and `glmUtils.get_timeseries_data`; all streams share the imaging frame clock | identical window, identical clock, and inputs/outputs are taken from exactly the same frames as the neural data | yes |\n   469→| (d) binning | no re-binning; everything at the ~15.5 Hz frame rate (paper: all time series sampled at ~15.5 Hz) | one frame per time bin, 64.4836 ms | yes |\n   470→| (e) input construction | `glmUtils.get_timeseries_data` builds trials/pos/speed/licks/rewarded per frame | same source variables; decoder inputs are the four required by the task (time from trial start, environment (= `morph`), lap index (= `trials`), previous-lap outcome (= `isreward` shifted by one)) | yes, restricted to the task spec |\n   471→| (f) output construction | `rel_pos` = distance from `rzone[i,0]` (`behavior.get_reward_zones`); `pos`; `speed`; binary `licks` (`licks[licks>1]=1`); `isreward` (`behavior.get_trial_types`); `rz label` | same variables, with the discretisation required by the task; distance is to the nearest point of the 50 cm zone (task asks for distance to *any* location in the zone) rather than to the zone start | yes, with the task-mandated discretisation |\n   472→\n   473→Differences and their reasons:\n   474→1. **No 2 cm/s speed threshold.** The reference drops sub-threshold samples for place-cell, GLM and decoder\n   475→   analyses. Here speed is an output with an explicit `< 2 cm/s` class and the decoder needs contiguous trial\n   476→   time series, so all within-trial samples are kept.\n   477→2. **Distance measured to the nearest point of the reward zone** (0 throughout the zone) instead of the\n   478→   reference RR position relative to the zone start, because the task specifies bins around 0 for the zone.\n   479→3. **Linear (not circular) distance**: the paper decoder used circular RR coordinates; the task specifies\n   480→   linear signed distance bins in cm.\n   481→4. **Extra exclusions**: 81 lick-error laps (the reference NaNs their lick values, which is not possible for a\n   482→   required output) and the first lap of each session (no previous-lap outcome).\n   483→5. **Extra neuron rule** for numerically unstable dF/F baselines (0.018% of cells), needed because the decoder\n   484→   applies PCA to raw event amplitudes.\n   485→\n   486→### Check 4: Key statistics comparison\n   487→See the table in Step 9. Highlights: 11 subjects, 152 sessions (14/mouse, m11 12), 12,216 complete laps in the\n   488→data vs 12,376 imaged trials quoted by the paper (the NWB release contains 160 fewer laps), 80.37 +/- 6.16 laps\n   489→per session vs 80.5 +/- 7.4 in the paper, 155-2341 curated cells per session vs 155-2172 quoted, exactly 81\n   490→lick-sensor-error laps as in the paper, 84.7% rewarded laps (paper: omissions on a ~15% subset), interneuron\n   491→exclusion 0.29% (paper 0.42 +/- 0.85%), frame period 64.4836 ms (paper 0.0645 s), reward zones at 80/200/320 cm\n   492→verified against the measured zone-entry position (median error 0.78 cm).\n   493→\n   494→### Check 5: Edge cases\n   495→- Frame-count mismatch between ophys and behaviour in 10 sessions: detected and handled by truncation; the\n   496→  affected sessions now convert and pass the independent sanity checks (m18 ses-13 above).\n   497→- `trial_start` at frame 0 would make the `[start-1, ...)` window invalid: verified never to occur.\n   498→- Trials never contain pre-synchronisation samples (`trial number` = -1, position = -500), non-scanning samples,\n   499→  or teleport-period samples (position = -50); asserted on all 152 sessions.\n   500→- Sessions with fewer than 2 usable trials would break decoder validation: the conversion drops such sessions\n   501→  (none occurred; the smallest session keeps 39 laps).\n   502→- Non-finite event values would fail format verification: each trial is checked with `np.all(np.isfinite(...))`\n   503→  before being stored (none were dropped).\n   504→- Laps beyond lap 30 in switch sessions get the post-switch zone; sessions with fewer than 30 laps (m4 ses-04,\n   505→  41 laps) are handled because the zone list is built with `min(CHANGE_TRIAL, n_trials)`.\n   506→- The last lap of a session is complete (paired teleport) in every session, so no partial lap is included.\n   507→\n   508→---\n   509→\n   510→## Step 11: Full Decoder Training\n   511→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   512→\n   513→Command: `python -u /app/train_decoder.py /app/converted_data.pkl --plot-samples`\n   514→(ran to completion on the GPU (NVIDIA L4); Definition check on the raw data: across all 12,216 laps there is **no** lap with a delivered reward but no\n   555→   reward-zone flag, and 52 laps with a zone flag but no reward (the reference `get_omission_trials` calls these\n   556→   lapsed trials, distinct from true omissions). So my `isreward` equals the reference\n   557→   `behavior.get_trial_types` rule and equals simply whether a reward was delivered.\n   558→2. `autoreward` is identically zero in every NWB file, so no extra information is available there.\n   559→3. Causal structure: on rewarded laps the reward is delivered on average 40.7% of the way through the lap\n   560→   (median 40.4%). Before that moment the animal has not yet experienced the outcome, so a frame-wise decoder\n   561→   cannot know it; the label is nevertheless defined for the whole lap because it is a per-trial variable.\n   562→4. Direct test: a 19-session decoder run (`diag_outcome_decode.py`) split validation frames by reward-zone\n   563→   distance bin. Balanced accuracy for `reward_outcome` was **0.531 before the reward zone** (distance bins 0-2)\n   564→   and **0.637 in/after the reward zone** (bins 3-6), versus 0.598 overall. The information is therefore present\n   565→   in CA1 activity only once the animal reaches the zone, exactly as expected for the physiology (the paper\n   566→   Fig. 6 contrasts rewarded vs omission trials *after* reward-zone entry). The modest overall number is a\n   567→   property of the task and of a frame-wise per-trial label, not a conversion error.\n   568→5. Class balance is 84.3% rewarded / 15.7% omitted, which is the true behavioural rate (paper: omissions on a\n   569→   ~15% subset of laps), so this is not a degenerate label either.\n   570→\n   571→### Check 2: Accuracy comparison to paper\n   572→Every decoding result in the paper (Fig. 3, Extended Data Fig. 3) is expressed as a circular-linear decode\n   573→score (mean cosine of the angular error) or as a z-score relative to a 100-shuffle null, for the decoding of\n   574→reward-relative position from selected cell classes (RR, track, non-RR remapping). The paper reports no\n   575→classification accuracy for any variable, and it never decodes position, speed, licking, reward-zone identity\n   576→or reward outcome. A numeric table comparing accuracies is therefore not possible. The qualitative expectation\n   577→from the paper - that reward-relative position is decodable well above chance from deconvolved CA1 events -\n   578→is reproduced (3.7x chance, the best of all outputs in relative terms).\n   579→\n   580→### Check 3: Train vs validation gap\n   581→| Output | Train | Validation | Train/Val |\n   582→|---|---|---|---|\n   583→| reward_zone_distance | 0.611 | 0.534 | 1.14 |\n   584→| position | 0.698 | 0.645 | 1.08 |\n   585→| speed | 0.630 | 0.583 | 1.08 |\n   586→| lick | 0.780 | 0.758 | 1.03 |\n   587→| reward_zone_location | 0.891 | 0.854 | 1.04 |\n   588→| reward_outcome | 0.798 | 0.590 | 1.35 |\n   589→All ratios are below 1.5, so there is no evidence of severe overfitting or data leakage; the largest gap\n   590→(reward_outcome, 1.35) is expected for a per-trial label with only ~79 labels per session (the network can\n   591→partly memorise training laps, but cannot do so for held-out laps).\n   592→\n   593→### Additional debugging checks performed\n   594→- Output values verified against the raw NWB data for three whole sessions, trial by trial (Step 10, Check 2).\n   595→- Temporal alignment verified visually (`processing_*_timeseries.png`, `processing_*_outputs.png`): the neural\n   596→  raster, position, speed and licks of a single trial are plotted on the same time axis and the trial\n   597→  boundaries coincide; also verified numerically because inputs, outputs and neural data are extracted from the\n   598→  identical frame index range.\n   599→- Output variability: no output is degenerate (the most imbalanced is reward_outcome at 84/16).\n   600→- Neural filtering follows the reference (iscell + speed-correlated interneurons) and was verified by exact\n   601→  recomputation of the kept-cell counts.\n   602→- Processing matches the reference dF/F + OASIS pipeline (verified by an independent re-implementation).\n   603→\n   604→### Issues Found and Resolved (all iterations)\n   605→1. **Ophys/behaviour frame-count mismatch** (10 m17/m18 sessions) crashed the first full run -> streams are now\n   606→   truncated to the common length; those sessions convert and pass sanity checks.\n   607→2. **Numerically exploding dF/F** for 25 cells whose maximin baseline collapses to ~0 -> those cells are\n   608→   excluded; dF/F range fell from [-931, 3013] to [-16.0, 37.2].\n   609→3. **Sample decoder log** initially only contained the tail of the run -> re-run and saved in full.\n   610→After each fix, the conversion, verification and decoder-training steps were re-run and all checks of Step 10\n   611→were repeated (39/39 independent sanity checks pass; verification reports no errors or warnings).\n   612→\n   613→---\n   614→\n   615→## Step 13: Documentation and Cleanup\n   616→**Status**: COMPLETE\n   617→\n   618→- [x] `README.md` created (dataset description, loading instructions, format specification, key statistics,\n   619→      processing summary and decoder performance).\n   620→- [x] `cache/` folder created with all exploration/validation scripts and `cache/README_CACHE.md` describing\n   621→      each file.\n   622→- [x] All required deliverables present in `/app`:\n   623→  `CONVERSION_NOTES.md`, `convert_data.py`, `converted_data.pkl` (9.47 GB), `sample_data.pkl`, `README.md`,\n   624→  `conversion_sample_out.txt`, `verification_sample_out.txt`, `train_decoder_sample_out.txt`,\n   625→  `conversion_full_out.txt`, `verification_full_out.txt`, `train_decoder_full_out.txt`.\n   626→- [x] Extra artefacts: `converted_data_session_stats.csv` and `sample_data_session_stats.csv` (per-session\n   627→      conversion statistics), `processing_m11_ses-04_*.png` and `processing_m19_ses-02_*.png`\n   628→      (`--show-processing` figures), `sample_trials.png` and `predictions.png` (decoder figures).\n   629→- [x] `__pycache__` removed.\n   630→\n   631→### Key decisions recap\n   632→1. Neural signal = OASIS-deconvolved events recomputed from NWB F/Fneu with the reference per-trial maximin\n   633→   dF/F pipeline (the stored `Deconvolved` series is suite2p deconvolution of raw F and does not match the\n   634→   reference processing).\n   635→2. No re-binning: one imaging frame (64.4836 ms) per time bin, matching the reference and preserving exact\n   636→   alignment between neural and behavioural streams.\n   637→3. Trials = complete laps with the reference window `[trial_start-1, teleport-1)`, aligned to trial start.\n   638→4. Curation = suite2p `iscell` + interneuron exclusion (dF/F-speed r > 0.5) + 25 numerically unstable cells;\n   639→   81 lick-sensor-error laps and the 152 first laps (undefined previous outcome) dropped.\n   640→5. the\nNWB conversion appears to drop a few incomplete laps (e.g. a final lap without a teleport). Every NWB session\nhas exactly matching numbers of trial_start and teleport events, so all 12,216 complete laps are used.\nThe max of 2341 cells/session (m18 day 3, 2-plane) slightly exceeds the paper range 155-2172; this is the pooled\ntwo-plane count and the paper range may have been computed per plane or on a subset of days.\n\n### Processing Details\n- **Temporal alignment**: VR behaviour is already interpolated onto imaging frame times (vr_align_to_2P in the\n  authors TwoPUtils repo); in the NWB files every behavioural series shares the imaging-frame timestamps.\n- **Trial window**: reference code (preprocessing.dff, glmUtils.get_timeseries_data) uses\n  [trial_start-1, teleport-1), i.e. from one frame before the trial-start flag up to one frame before teleport.\n- **dF/F**: per-trial maximin baseline: neuropil subtraction (0.7 x Fneu, per-trial neuropil mean added back),\n  Gaussian smooth (sigma = 15 frames), minimum filter (300 frames ~ 20 s), maximum filter (300 frames),\n  dF/F = (F - baseline)/|baseline|, then Gaussian smoothing with sigma = 2 frames (~0.129 s).\n- **Events**: OASIS deconvolution of smoothed dF/F per trial (suite2p.extraction.dcnv.oasis, batch 2000,\n  tau = 0.7 s from the suite2p ops in the repo notebook, fs = frame_rate/n_planes = 15.5078 Hz).\n- **Reward-relative position**: distance from the start of the active reward zone (rzone[i,0]) per trial.\n- **GLM/decoder variables**: position (0-450 cm), RR position, rewarded binary (0 until reward delivery then 1\n  until end of trial), speed, acceleration, smoothed binary licks.\n\n### Curation Steps\n**Neuron curation rules**:\n1. suite2p manual curation: keep ROIs with iscell[:,0] == 1 (the manual curation of the Methods).\n2. Exclude putative interneurons: Pearson r > 0.5 between the cell dF/F and running speed (within-trial samples).\n3. Planes are pooled for multi-plane mice (m17, m18).\n\n**Trial curation rules**:\n1. Only complete laps, defined by paired trial_start/teleport flags; samples restricted to [start-1, stop-1).\n2. Lick-sensor error trials (>30% of frames with cumulative lick count > 2, n = 81) have lick values set to NaN in\n   the reference; because lick is a required decoder output here, these trials cannot provide a valid lick label,\n   so they are dropped entirely (see Step 5).\n\n### Decoders Trained (paper)\n| Decoded variable | Accuracy |\n| Reward-relative (circular) position from deconvolved events of RR / track / non-RR remapping cells (Fig. 3) | reported as a mean cosine decode score (1 = perfect, 0 = chance) and as a z-score versus a 100-shuffle null, not as % classification accuracy. Fig. 3a shows a single fold of an example session; group means are well above shuffle. No classification accuracies are reported in the paper, so no direct numeric comparison with this task accuracy is possible. |\n\n---\n\n## Step 4: Check for Consistency\n**Status**: COMPLETE\n\nI cross-checked the reference code, the NWB data, and the paper text.
